# APPLICATION MANIFEST — Chinese Ayah Companion, Sweep of 2026-10-12

Patch Set 1 application units for the Chinese Ayah Companion (Robert Tilford’s Recital, ZH lane). Built from `PATCH-INDEX.md` (including Addendum 2) and the four lane reports, verified verse-by-verse against the two masters. **This manifest changes no manuscript.** Each row is one verse with ONE proposed combined final Chinese text; approval is by class or by row, at Robert’s word.

- **CN anchor (current text quoted from):** `~/workspace/robert-tilford-quran-staging/translations/chinese/ayah-companion-manuscript-ZH.md` (house-designated published anchor)
- **EN warrant (governing text):** `~/workspace/original-recital/files/original-recital-FULL.txt`, sha256 `c47105f2…` — Robert’s English Recital is the sole source; where it differs from any other translation, the English governs.
- **Standards:** `~/workspace/ayah-chinese/GLOSSARY-ZH.md`; the Patch Index governs over the glossary table where the two diverge (noted per row).
- **Unresolved at the source (standing note):** no local ZH copy matches the archive.org published hash `bb7ee6bd…`; staging = GitHub mirror = Drive pack at `2030fe97…`. The patch phase must close with one canonical file and three-way byte verification (Welsh pattern).

## Header — counts

- **Total application rows (distinct verses): 724** — main manifest 708 rows + P-11 supplement 16 rows. Verses appearing in both scopes carry ONE merged row in the main tables (2:144, 2:158, 2:196) and are cross-listed in the supplement introduction.
- **Rows by disposition:** patch 719 · preserve 5 (4:51, 20:4, 26:192, 56:80, 69:43).
- **Verification:** context-verified 485 · candidate 239. Candidate = enumerated by a stated pairing rule at manifest build and read in full, but not individually quoted in a lane report, or a proposal whose carrier choice is flagged in the row. Nothing in a candidate row is guessed silently — the row says why.

- P-02 split (Addendum 2 §3): P-02a plain-definite 61 rows · P-02b possessive/attributive 25 rows (candidate — referent check at application review).
- P-03: 248 rows. The index’s ≈177 was a floor, as instructed: clause-level pairing across surahs 3–114 (plus the Surah 2 samples) yields the true population. Every proposal was generated clause-by-clause and residue-scanned — surviving singular 我 instances in P-03 proposals are human speech (prophets, Job, Jesus, the praying man), individually confirmed.
- P-04 split (Addendum 2 §2): signs family (CN 启示/奇迹/节文/经文/教训 for EN "signs"), verses family (reverse swap), reminder family (3 rows + 74:31’s instance + 74:54’s combined row), sending-down family (4 preserve rows). No blanket substitution was made anywhere.
- Corrective-particle census (Addendum 2 §6): 135 EN corrective instances book-wide ("No —" / "Yes —", incl. sentence-internal). 38 manifest rows repair the dropped/flattened ones; the remainder are carried in the CN （不然/不/绝不/其实/事实上/是的/有/当然 and equivalents), including 58:18/58:19/58:22 （确实） — except two carried-in-substance cases logged in the exclusions below.
- Refrains enumerated per occurrence (Addendum 2 §8): Surah 54 refrain at 54:17, 54:22, 54:32, 54:40 (four rows, one unified repair). Surah 55’s refrain is a P-11e held item — excluded.

## Verses deliberately excluded as candidates-not-proposed

These were examined and are NOT rows. Listing them keeps the counts reconcilable.

1. **P-09f fasiq pair — 2:26, 2:99** （犯罪者 vs 作恶者 for "the defiant"): held with P-11e per the index (low confidence each); carrier choice is Robert’s.
2. **Lane D P-03 items not sustained against the masters (24):** 58:12, 59:13, 61:9, 65:1, 72:19, 74:44, 76:16, 76:30, 77:30, 77:34, 77:50, 84:13, 84:20, 87:7, 87:9, 88:22, 95:6, 96:6, 96:8, 96:15, 98:2, 103:6, 108:1, 89:28 — at each, the EN carries no divine We-token and the CN no flattened 我 (several are imperatives, refrains, or human speech; 103:6 does not exist — Surah 103 has three verses; 108:1 already reads 我们）. Lane D’s six sustained items (73:12, 73:16, 75:17, 75:19, 76:23, 77:23) are rows.
3. **P-04 lane items not sustained (5):** 3:190, 10:5, 10:6, 30:21, 45:3 — the anchor CN already reads 迹象 at each; the lane quotes do not match the anchor text.
4. **Ghost items (2):** 7:54 （余荫/门框 — neither word exists in the anchor manuscript) and 35:6 （仿效 — not in the anchor text). Lane B quotes not sustained; excluded. (98:8 was partially sustained — Eden element only — and is a modified candidate row.)
5. **P-03 candidates adjudicated clean (the pairing pool’s rejects):** verses whose CN divine clauses already read 我们， or whose 我 is the EN’s own singular (divine "I" passages, prophets’ and believers’ speech): 2:38, 2:125, 2:133, 2:249, 2:259, 3:52, 3:81, 4:18, 5:12, 5:44, 5:111, 6:114, 6:130, 6:151, 7:59, 7:142, 7:143, 7:145, 7:146, 7:155, 7:156, 10:15, 10:90, 11:10, 11:25, 11:37, 11:54, 12:72, 13:30, 13:36, 14:14, 14:37, 14:40, 14:41, 17:2, 17:61, 17:101, 18:50, 18:52, 18:56, 18:102, 18:109, 19:21, 19:77, 20:40, 20:77, 20:81, 20:124, 21:25, 21:29, 21:87, 21:105, 22:26, 22:72, 23:109, 29:8, 32:13, 34:45, 35:40, 39:49, 40:43, 41:47, 42:15, 50:27, 50:45, 54:37, 60:4, 72:1, 78:40.
6. **3:13 (signs pairing false hit):** the CN 教训 answers EN "a lesson", not "signs"; the sign itself is correctly 迹象. No fault.
7. **37:150** — needs no change of its own: its orphan closing quote becomes legitimate once 37:149’s premature closure is repaired (see the 37:149 row).
8. **Corrective instances carried in substance (2):** 2:260 (Abraham’s "Yes —" answered by 信， "I believe, but…") and 3:125 （够了 for "Yes —") — graded variation, not drops; no rows.
9. **45:25 as a verses-family item** — CN already reads 节文； the verse is a P-03 row only.

| 2:196 | P-09, P-11c | 你们当为真主完成朝觐和副朝。但如果你们被阻，就献力所能及的牺牲。在牺牲到达其地之前，不要剃你们的头。你们中患病或头部有伤的人，就以斋戒、施舍或敬拜赎。当你们安全时：谁把副朝持续到朝觐，就献力所能及的牺牲。但如果无力，就在朝觐期间斋戒三天，回来后再斋戒七天，共十天。这是为家不在禁寺附近的人。敬畏真主，知道真主的惩罚是严厉的。 | And complete the pilgrimage and the visit for God. | 4:34 | P-10 | 男人是妇女的保护者和供养者，因为真主使他们中一些人优于另一些，并因为他们从财产中支出。善良的妇女是顺从的，守护真主让她们守护的。至于你们怕不忠的，劝告她们，离弃她们于床上，然后打她们。但如果她们顺从你们，不要对她们寻衅。真主是崇高的、伟大的。 | Men are the protectors and maintainers of women, because God favored some of them over others, and because they spend from their wealth. But if they obey you, seek no way against them. God is the Most High, the Great. | 男人是妇女的保护者和供养者，因为真主使他们中一些人优于另一些，并因为他们从财产中支出。善良的妇女是顺从的，守护真主所守护的幽玄。至于你们怕不忠的，劝告她们，离弃她们于床上，然后打她们。但如果她们顺从你们，不要对她们寻衅。真主是崇高的、伟大的。 | omission — "guarding the unseen that God has guarded": the unseen （幽玄） dropped, leaving a circular paraphrase | n/a — source-fidelity: the Recital EN governs | — | patch | context-verified NOTE: The repair uses the CN’s own standing 幽玄 carrier; Ruling 4 on 幽玄 itself is accounted in the supplement/appendix and is not changed by this row. | pending Robert | — |
| 4:43 | P-09 | 信道的人们啊！当你们醉酒时不要接近礼拜，以便你们知道所说的；也不要在不洁状态——除非你们旅行——直到你们沐浴。如果你们患病、或旅行、或你们中有人从厕所出来、或你们接触了妇女，而找不到水，就找干净的土抹你们的脸和手。真主是宽恕的、至恕的。 | Believers — do not come near the prayer while you are drunk, until you know what you are saying — nor unclean, except travelers passing through, until you wash. God is the Pardoner, the Forgiver. | 信道的人们啊！当你们醉酒时不要接近礼拜，以便你们知道所说的；也不要在不洁状态——除非你们旅行——直到你们沐浴。如果你们患病、或旅行、或你们中有人从厕所出来、或你们接触了妇女，而找不到水，就找洁净的土抹你们的脸和手。真主是宽恕的、至恕的。 | acceptable variation — "clean earth" rendered 干净的土； the fixed phrase (as correctly used at 5:6) is 洁净的土 | glossary-noncompliant — GLOSSARY-ZH fixes the carrier | — | patch | context-verified | pending Robert | — |

| 5:3 | P-10 | 对你们禁止的是：自死物、血、猪肉，以及为真主以外的名义而宰的牲畜；还有被勒死的、被打死的、摔死的、被角抵死的、被野兽撕碎的——除了你们及时宰杀的——以及在祭坛上宰的牲畜；还有掣签。那是悖逆。今天，不信道者已对你们的宗教绝望，所以不要怕他们，要怕我。今天我已为你们成全你们的宗教，为你们完成我的恩典，为你们选择伊斯兰为宗教。但谁因饥饿所迫而无意犯罪——真主是至恕的、至慈的。 | Forbidden to you are carrion, blood, pork, and what was dedicated to other than God; the strangled, the beaten to death, the fallen, the gored, what the beast ate — except what you slaughter properly — and what was slaughtered on altars; and that you draw lots with arrows. That is defiance. Today those who disbelieve have given up on your religion — so do not fear them, but fear Me. Today I have completed your religion for you, and finished My blessing on you, and chosen surrender as your religion. Whoever is forced by hunger, not leaning toward sin — God is Forgiving, Merciful. | 对你们禁止的是：自死物、血、猪肉，以及为真主以外的名义而宰的牲畜；还有被勒死的、被打死的、摔死的、被角抵死的、被野兽撕碎的——除了你们及时宰杀的——以及在祭坛上宰的牲畜；还有掣签。那是悖逆。今天，不信道者已对你们的宗教绝望，所以不要怕他们，要怕我。今天我已为你们成全你们的宗教，为你们完成我的恩典，为你们选择归顺为宗教。但谁因饥饿所迫而无意犯罪——真主是至恕的、至慈的。 | contradiction — Robert’s EN renders the word descriptively ("surrender" / "submitters"); the CN restores the proper nouns 伊斯兰/穆斯林， answering the tradition instead of the Recital | n/a — source-fidelity: the Recital EN governs | — | patch | context-verified | pending Robert | — |
| 5:6 | P-07 | 信道的人们啊！当你们起身礼拜时，洗你们的脸，洗你们的手和手臂到肘，抹你们的头，洗你们的脚到踝。 *[有些读法是抹到脚为止；这里印刷的读法是洗。]* 如果你们处于不洁的状态，就洁净你们自己。如果你们患病，或在旅行中，或你们中有人从厕所回来，或你们接触过妇女，而找不到水，那就用洁净的土，以它抹你们的脸和手。真主不想给你们增加负担，但他想洁净你们，想完成他对你们的恩典，以便你们感谢。 | Believers — when you rise for the prayer, wash your faces and your hands to the elbows, and wipe your heads, and your feet to the ankles. If you are unclean, purify yourselves. If you are sick, or traveling, or one of you comes from the toilet, or you have touched women, and you find no water — then reach for clean earth and wipe your faces and your hands with it. # P-11 SUPPLEMENT — the four locked rulings (Robert, 2026-10-12)

Ruling 1 (1A, riba): 利息 kept — no repair rows; the five verses are do-not-touch controls (Appendix B). Ruling 2 (2A, taghoot per-verse): four repair rows + one preserve row below; controls at 5:60, 16:36, 39:17 in Appendix D. Ruling 3 (3A, sites phrase-by-phrase): eleven supplement rows below + three repairs merged into main-manifest rows 2:144, 2:158, 2:196 (one row per verse — the proposed texts there are the combined finals); discharged site verses in Appendix A. Ruling 4 (4A, al-Ghayb): 幽玄 kept everywhere — no repair rows; all occurrences are do-not-touch controls (Appendix C).

| 2:187 | P-11c | 斋戒之夜与你们的妻子交合对你们是许可的。她们是你们的衣服，你们是她们的衣服。真主知道你们曾欺骗自己，但他转向你们，宽恕了你们。所以现在亲近她们，寻求真主为你们规定的，吃喝，直到黎明的白线与黑线能分辨。然后完成斋戒直到夜晚。但当你们在清真寺静修时，不要亲近她们。这些是真主的界限，不要接近它们。真主就这样向人们阐明他的迹象，以便他们敬畏。 | Allowed for you on the night of fasting is intimacy with your women. They are a garment for you, and you are a garment for them. God knew you were betraying yourselves, so He relented toward you and pardoned you. So now be with them, and seek what God wrote for you. # APPENDIX — DO-NOT-TOUCH CONTROLS

Verses listed here were verified correct or ruled preserved. Application tooling must skip them; they exist so a class-wide pass never "repairs" a control by pattern-matching.

## A. Sites — discharged / authorized (Ruling 3: the EN itself names the place)

- 禁寺 authorized (EN "the Sacred Mosque"): 5:2, 8:34, 9:7, 9:19, 9:28, 17:1, 22:25, 48:25, 48:27.
- 天房 authorized (EN "the Kaaba"): 5:95, 5:97.
- 清真寺 authorized (EN generic "a mosque" / "mosques"): 9:107, 9:108, 22:40.
- 52:4 — 天房 for EN "the frequented House": graded VARIATION under the ruling; carrier defensible, stands.

## B. Riba — Ruling 1 controls （利息 stands)

- 2:275, 2:276, 2:278, 3:130, 4:161 — EN "usury" → 利息， kept by locked Ruling 1A. (2:276 carries a main-manifest P-10 repair to its 施舍 clause only; its 利息 is untouched. 30:39’s 高利贷 answers EN "increase", not "usury", and is likewise untouched.)

## C. Al-Ghayb — Ruling 4 controls （幽玄 stands at every occurrence)

- 2:3, 2:33, 3:44, 5:109, 6:59, 9:78, 10:20, 13:9, 16:77, 18:26, 23:92, 32:6, 34:3, 34:14, 34:48, 39:46, 49:18, 52:41, 53:35, 59:22, 62:8, 64:18, 72:26, 81:24 — 24 verses containing 幽玄， all kept by locked Ruling 4A.

## D. Taghoot — Ruling 2 controls

- 5:60 （偶像崇拜者 for "worshipers of false gods"), 16:36 （偶像 for "the false gods"), 39:17 （崇拜偶像 for "the worship of idols") — CN already matches the EN per-verse; preserved. 4:51 is a preserve ROW in the supplement (variation logged).

## E. Other standing controls (from the lane adjudications)

- 天课 correct: 9:5, 9:71, 23:4, 27:3, 31:4, 33:33, 41:7, 58:13, 73:20. · 施舍 correct for alms/charity: 4:92, 28:54.
- 以物配主者 correct: 12:106, 12:108, 15:94, 16:35, 16:120, 23:59, 31:13, 48:6, 61:9. · 多神教徒 authorized where the EN itself reads "polytheists" (e.g. 5:82 and the Surah 9 treaty verses).
- 确信 discharged (EN reads "certain / certainty"): 5:50, 6:75, 13:2, 15:99, 27:3, 27:14, 74:31.
- 这部经典 already present and correct: 2:2, 10:37, 32:2, 39:1, 39:2, 39:41, 40:2, 45:2, 46:2 (45:29’s demonstrative is also correct — its fault is possessive person only, repaired under P-03). · 一部经典 correct for indefinite "a Book": 14:1, 21:10, 27:1, 46:4.
- Traveler control: 8:41 （旅行者 already correct). · Michael/Talut controls: none needed — the classes are exhausted by their rows.

## F. Build notes for the application phase

- The census quote-balance gate defect (curly quotes outside its extraction class) is a tooling bug in `census.py`, disclosed during the sweep; quote parity for this manifest was checked by direct count on every proposed text. Repairs that change balance do so deliberately and are noted (2:104, 2:222, 17:23, 36:22, 37:149).
- Apply by approved class; after each class, re-run the pairing greps for that class as a regression pass, then re-anchor the hash and close with the three-way byte verification (local = staging/GitHub = archive.org).
- Edition label on completion: "Revision 2026-10-12 — Patch Set 1" (per the adopted completion definition).
